\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{cxd4}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{cxd4} (\texttt{c5d4}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#3854} (ply 17) [structural distance $d$=103, QAP obj=104]: the master played \texttt{Nfd7}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nf3} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{P}f7 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{B}c8 as their \emph{N}b8; transferred move \texttt{c5d4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0001\#1514} (ply 18) [structural distance $d$=94, QAP obj=102]: the master played \texttt{cxd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}a2 as their \emph{N}b1, our \emph{p}e5 as their \emph{B}f1; transferred move \texttt{c5d4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#3855} (ply 17) [structural distance $d$=113, QAP obj=77]: the master played \texttt{Bc8}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bf4} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{P}d5 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{P}h7 as their \emph{N}b8; transferred move \texttt{c5d4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 3 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{cxd4} (summed QAP objective 283, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
\end{figure}
